[Unit]
Description=DLT Daemon

[Service]
Type=simple
ExecStartPre=/bin/bash -c 'if [ ! -d /data/dlt_logs ]; then mkdir -p /data/dlt_l
ExecStart=/usr/bin/dlt-daemon -c /etc/dlt.conf
Restart=always
StartLimitInterval=10min
StartLimitBurst=10
User=root
Group=root
StandardOutput=journal
StandardError=journal

[Install]
WantedBy=sysinit.target

[Unit]
Description=DLT Daemon

[Service]
Type=simple
ExecStartPre=/bin/bash -c 'if [ ! -d /data/dlt_logs ]; then mkdir -p /data/dlt_l
ExecStart=/usr/bin/dlt-daemon -c /etc/dlt.conf
Restart=always
StartLimitInterval=10min
StartLimitBurst=10
User=root
Group=root
StandardOutput=journal
StandardError=journal

[Install]
WantedBy=sysinit.ta
